# Skills · A · part 38

898 entries, aeko-ga4 to aerospace-engineering-classification-expert. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **aeko-ga4**: Shows GA4 traffic and impact through either the customer's own official GA4 connector for free or AEKO's account-gated GA4 and Measure path. Emits normalized traffic and impact rows, including explicit unavailable rows when no source is connected. Use for traffic, ecommerce impact, or GA4 setup. ([AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin](https://github.com/AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/aeko-ga4) · ★1)
- **aeko-manage-prompts**: Discovers, tracks, reviews, organizes, and untracks AEKO prompts; manages saved views and Context memories; and handles review-derived suggested prompts. Use for prompt quota, tracking changes, review-suggested questions, saved views, or Context curation. Writes require explicit selection and gates. ([AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin](https://github.com/AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/aeko-manage-prompts) · ★1)
- **aeko-message-audit**: Audits literal claims in active Meta, Google Ads, and TikTok creative, hard-deduplicates and spend-ranks them for free, then checks landing pages and optionally AEKO-owned content and monitored answers. Use for unsupported ad claims, paid-to-owned message gaps, and claims AI misses or gives to competitors. Requires at least one customer's official ad connector. ([AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin](https://github.com/AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/aeko-message-audit) · ★1)
- **aeko-openai-ads-reporting**: Produces an account-gated, client/CMO-ready OpenAI Ads performance report for one explicit ad account over completed account-local days. Ranks top and bottom campaigns, ad groups, ads, and products by CTR, CPC, spend, impressions, and clicks; adds stored conversion coverage; optionally folds in organic AI visibility; writes a local Markdown report; and remains read-only and schedulable. Use for depth on AEKO-operated OpenAI Ads, not a cross-platform glance. ([AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin](https://github.com/AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/aeko-openai-ads-reporting) · ★1)
- **aeko-openai-budget-shift**: Account-gated AEKO workflow, read-only for budgets, that reads OpenAI Ads campaign performance and recommends budget shifts: a ranked list of from → to moves with amounts, reasoning, and risks, which the merchant applies in the AEKO dashboard. It never changes a budget; AEKO campaign budgets are set only in the dashboard. Scores efficiency proxies (CTR/CPC/spend; ROAS not available). Optional waste-cutting pauses clearly wasteful campaigns, ad groups, or ads only after explicit confirmation. Unattended runs only recommend and never pause or resume anything. ([AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin](https://github.com/AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/aeko-openai-budget-shift) · ★1)
- **aeko-openai-compose-ads**: Account-gated AEKO workflow that composes flexible, cross-product OpenAI Ads ad groups from a store's contextual reviews. Samples a bounded set of contextual reviews for one domain, clusters them by a shared shopper-situation facet (e.g. several products all matching '민감성 피부' or occasion '선물'), composes ONE broader context-hint set per cluster, and creates an ad group with one ad per product — all PAUSED for review. This is the agentic step beyond the dashboard's one-review→one-ad flow. ([AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin](https://github.com/AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/aeko-openai-compose-ads) · ★1)
- **aeko-openai-guardrails**: Interactive, account-gated AEKO workflow for OpenAI Ads pacing rules. Anchors thresholds on observed merchant data, creates rules disabled, requires a successful blast-radius preview before arming, safely edits enabled rules by disarming first, and confirm-gates both directions of the account-wide switch. Bounded by hourly reporting; entity resume is always a separate server-gated action. ([AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin](https://github.com/AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/aeko-openai-guardrails) · ★1)
- **aeko-pdp-audit**: Checks whether one product page is ready to be cited by AI. Shows the exact text available without reading pixels, then reads stacked detail images with native vision to recover trapped product facts. Works from a URL or local HTML file with no AEKO account or connector. ([AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin](https://github.com/AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/aeko-pdp-audit) · ★1)
- **aeko-pdp-build**: Turns facts recovered by aeko-pdp-audit into a responsive, citation-ready product description plus non-duplicating Product/FAQPage JSON-LD. Accepts the audit's aeko_pdp_image_facts/v1 block or a product URL. Free and zero-account: it produces paste-ready output without connecting to or writing to a store. ([AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin](https://github.com/AEKO-Intelligence/aeko-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/aeko-pdp-build) · ★1)
- **aeko-publish-content**: Interactive publisher and takedown workflow for backend-saved content variations. Detects live overwrite risk across every row, names the target URL, and requires same-turn human confirmation. Reads approval threads first, then decisions and blackouts, pulls normalized sources, deduplicates proposals, and delivers a report. ([aliabdalla-maker/aepx-core](https://github.com/aliabdalla-maker/aepx-core/tree/HEAD/skills/aepx-svc-discovery) · ★1)
- **aepx-svc-gateway**: AEP-X Gateway.
